*Deaths/Tick derived: (initial + recruited − remaining) / ticks — assumes units only leave via combat death. Supply-Blocked = units whose supply delivery was denied by the player's funds / manpower pool (FCFS gate; see city:unit ratio caveat).

Network Phase

Sub-phases of the per-tick network phase (splice encoder on in the benchmark). net = the whole tick_system!-wrapped phase (snapshot + delta + encode + baseline commit); the sub-rows are the measured parts, so their sum is ≤ net. send is always 0: the headless benchmark encodes in memory and models no outbox/WS bridge. ms/Tick = average over all 4500 measured ticks.
